Let's carefully derive:
b_k = ∑_{i=max} a_i a_{k-i}.
For derivative d/da_j: terms where i=j gives a_{k-j}; term where k-i=j gives i=k-j, also gives a_j. These are same term if j=k-j -> k=2j, one term a_j^2 derivative 2a_j. If both conditions valid and distinct, derivative = a_{k-j}+a_{j}. So gradient is a reversed/shifted plus symmetric. In code previous `_gradient_at_lags`: for k, they did grad[j] += 2*w*f[k-j] for valid j. That seems assumes each pair counted twice? Let's check for convolution b_k = ∑_{i} f_i f_{k-i}. The mixing step with g encourages directions that have large total mass but low cross-convolution with f at peaks. This can reduce f*f. We can approximate g by solving linear inequalities via **projected gradient / multiplicative weights** instead of full LP.

Given f and M=bmax_f. We want g≥0, sum max, f*g (cross-convolution) ≤M. Since sum g maybe target. This is like finding a "dual" sequence. We can solve using multiplicative weights:
- Start g = uniform or f.
- Iterate while violating constraints: for each k where (f*g)_k close to M, reduce g_j that contribute to that lag. Contribution to (f*g)_k wrt g_j is f_{k-j}. So gradient is shifted f (same as before but with f). Update g_j *= exp(-η f_{k-j}). This is similar to our optimizer with a=f and weights on cross-correlation, but target M maybe current. Then mix.

Let's focus on implementing a high-performing multiplicative gradient descent with:
- Dense softmax weights over all lags, adaptive temperature.
- Line search with surrogate and exact score.
- Log domain and optional L-BFGS polishing.

We need produce code not too long but robust. Let's design helper functions top-level.

Helper functions:
- `_fft_len(n, m)`: next power of two >= m.
- `_b_max(a, L)`: compute b max via rfft.
- `_score_from_bmax(bmax,n)`: 2*n*bmax (assumes sum=1).
- `_normalize(a)`: max 0, sum 1.
- `_weights_from_b(b, sharp, rng?)`: create λ over lags.
   Option: use `t = b / (b.max()+eps)`; `w = t**p` for p? But b all positive ~1/n; p large focuses top. Or `q=(b-b.min())/(b.max()-b.min()+eps)`, `w=np.power(q, p)`. If p=1 uniform over range. To ensure sum moderate. But if q zero many, w small. We can normalize to sum 1. Or with temperature: `w=np.exp(temp*q - temp*qmax)` where qmax max. Sharp parameter `temp`. I'll use `q = (b - b.min()) / (b.max() - b.min() + eps)` and `w = np.exp(sharp * q)`, normalize. `sharp` from 1 to 20. Since q in [0,1], max weight exp(sharp). Need not be top only. Alternatively, use top-K plus tail: weights for top 64 with exponential rank; but dense FFT simpler.
   However using range normalization each iteration: if b has peaks separated, q emphasizes relative. Good.
- `_grad_weights(a, w, A, L)`: H=rfft(w,L), return 2*irfft(conj(A)*H,L)[:n].real.
- `_multiplicative_update(a, g, eta)`: center g by weighted mean (or max) to prevent overflow. `sg = g - np.dot(a,g)`; delta = -eta*sg; shift max; a_new=a*exp(delta); sum; if sum bad fallback.
- `_try_step(a, g, eta, bmax_cur, L, n)`: update, compute bmax_new, if improved accept; else halve. Return a,bmax,eta,accepted.
   We need avoid too many FFTs: one per trial. We can do at most 6 halvings. If initial eta small, maybe increase. We can maintain eta.
- `_opt_mirror(a, L, n, deadline, rng, sharp_start, sharp_end, eta, etc)`: main loop.
   Variables: a (sum1), bmax.
   best_a, best_bmax.
   momentum m = zeros.
   Iters until time. Each iter:
      if time expired break.
      A=rfft(a,L)
      B=irfft(A*A,L)[:2n-1]
      bmax=B.max()
      if bmax<best: update.
      weights = _weights_from_b(B, sharp)
      H=rfft(weights,L)
      G=2*irfft(conj(A)*H,L)[:n].real
      center=G - ΣaG
      apply momentum: Gm = G + beta*m? Need scale. m = beta*m + (1-beta)*G? Then Guse = Gm? In mirror previous, `grad_eff = grad + beta*m`; m updated `grad+beta*m`. That accumulates. We can use `m = beta*m + (1-beta)*G`; `Geff = G + beta*m`? Simpler: `m = beta*m + (1-beta)*G`; use `Gcenter` and `mcenter`? Since m should be centered too. We'll do `m = beta*m + (1-beta)*G`; `Geff = G + beta*m`; center Geff.
      eta schedule: start eta=eta0 maybe 1.0? Since G centered dynamic range maybe O(1/n) ~1e-4. Use eta_dim = eta_factor / (np.abs(Geff).max()+eps). eta_factor from 2 to 20? Let's estimate: If Geff range ~1e-4, eta=1/range=10000, log change ~O(eta*range)=O(1). Good. Step factor maybe 2-10. We'll set `step = eta_factor / (Geff.max()-Geff.min()+1e-12)`, with eta_factor tuned (e.g., 5). This means the maximum log multiplier is exp(step*range)=e^factor. Too large if factor 5. Could overshoot. Maybe factor 1 or 2. Previous step/span=0.8. Use factor 0.8 base.
      Update with eta maybe `step`. But if using factor over range, step = factor/range; delta range=factor. exp(5)~148, okay. We can choose factor=2.
      New a. try line search: evaluate bmax_new. If < bmax*(1-1e-12) accept, increase factor by 1.02, reset fail. Else halve factor until improvement or min. Since bmax is the objective (2n constant). We can also track surrogate F=Σ weights*B? But exact.
      If no improvement within e.g., 5 halvings, accept current? Maybe if eta too small, factor can reduce? We can increase later.
      Periodically perturb: if stagnation (no best improvement for `patience` evals), apply smooth random multiplicative perturbation to a or x. E.g., add low-pass noise: `a *= exp(noise)` where noise = random FFT with small magnitudes, or structured sine. Then reset best? Keep best separate. Also reduce current a to perturbed but optimize.

   Return best.

Let's design optimizer more concretely.

Pseudo:
```
def _optimize_sequence(a0, n, L, deadline, rng, label=''):
    a = _norm(a0)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_bmax=_bmax(a,L); best_score=2*n*best_bmax
    m=np.zeros(n)
    beta=0.6 # momentum
    sharp=4.0
    sharp_end=60.0
    step_factor=1.0
    max_factor=20.0
    consecutive_fail=0
    no_improve=0
    eval_count=0
    it=0
    while time < deadline:
        it+=1
        progress = min(1, eval_count/5000)
        temp = sharp + (sharp_end-sharp)*progress
        # if randomized occasional higher temp
        A=rfft(a,L)
        B=irfft(A*A,L)[:2n-1]; bmax=B.max()
        if bmax < best_bmax*(1-1e-12): update best
        # compute weights
        if bmax <=0: break
        # temperature over B/Bmax
        z = (temp * B / bmax)
        # subtract max for exp
        w = np.exp(z - z.max())
        wsum=w.sum(); if wsum<=0 fallback uniform
        w/=wsum
        H=rfft(w,L)
        G=2*irfft(conj(A)*H,L)[:n].real
        # center
        G -= G @ a ? Use np.dot(a,G)
        if it%? compute g_range = G.max()-G.min()
        if g_range < 1e-300: break
        m = beta*m + (1-beta)*G
        Guse = G + beta*m
        Guse -= Guse @ a
        g_range = Guse.max()-Guse.min()
        eta = step_factor / g_range
        # update candidates with decreasing eta
        accepted=False
        for trial in range(8):
            a_try = _mult(a, Guse, eta)
            A_try=rfft(a_try,L); B_try=irfft(A_try*A_try,L)[:2n-1]
            b_try=B_try.max()
            F_cur = np.dot(w, B)  # current weighted sum (B unchanged)
            F_try = np.dot(w, B_try)
            # Accept if weighted sum decreased sufficiently OR bmax decreased
            if F_try < F_cur*(1-1e-9) and b_try < bmax*(1+1e-9):
                 # ensure not worsening max too much? maybe allow small bmax increase if F improves
                 a=a_try; accepted=True; break
            # if bmax improves even if F slightly up:
            if b_try < bmax*(1-1e-10):
                 a=a_try; accepted=True; best update; break
            eta *=0.5
        if not accepted:
            # maybe try smaller or perturb
            no_improve +=1
            if no_improve > 30:
                a = _smooth_perturb(best_a? or a, rng, scale depending)
                m*=0.2
                no_improve=0
                step_factor = max(0.3, step_factor*0.7)
            else:
                step_factor *=0.9
        else:
            consecutive_fail=0
            if bmax < best: step_factor *=1.05 if <max
            else step_factor *=0.95
    return best_a,best_bmax
```
Issues:
- `F_cur = dot(w,B)` computed each iter. Since w sum normalized. F_cur ~ average top B ~bmax. Good.
- Accept if F_try < F_cur. But because w from current B, a new point may reduce F but bmax same. Good.
- If no line search success due to nonconvex, we may stagnate. Increase step or perturb.
- We should evaluate official occasionally? Not needed; internal bmax exact. But due to FFT rounding maybe small; final official.

Maybe use exact subgradient of bmax rather than weighted sum? Dense weights good.

Can we compute B and gradient using A once. For line search, each trial recompute A_try and B_try. Could reuse if eta not too large? Not.

Maybe do a **fixed eta + periodic full score** without line search to save FFT? But line search ensures progress. We can limit 4 halvings; each iter 2-5 B computations. FFT cost okay.

Potential performance: n=6121, L=16384. rfft ~ maybe 0.15ms, irfft 0.15ms. Each iter with 3 trials: 3* (rfft+irfft for B) plus one gradient FFT (rfft w + irfft) + main A. So ~6 FFTs ~1ms. 100k iters ~100s. Good.

Momentum: Since we line search and update weights, using momentum with G centered may help. But if F nonconvex, momentum could accumulate. Use beta 0.3-0.6. We can reset on perturbation.

Perturbation design:
```
def _smooth_perturb(a, rng, scale, n):
    # generate low-pass Gaussian noise
    order = min(n//2+1, 50)
    freqs = np.arange(order)
    amp = np.zeros(order)
    # power-law 1/f to smooth
    amp[1:] = rng.normal(0,1,size=order-1) / np.sqrt(freqs[1:] + 1)
    amp[0]=0
    noise = np.fft.irfft(amp * np.exp? need phase) 
```
Simpler: `noise = np.zeros(n); for k in range(1,20): noise += rng.normal()*np.sin(2π k j/n + phase)`. O(n*20)=120k, infrequent ok. Then `a_try = a * np.exp(scale * noise); norm`. scale maybe 0.2-1.0.
Also structured: multiply by exp(alpha*j/n) or sine. But smooth noise enough.

def _make_starts(n, rng, existing=None):
    j=np.arange(n); t=j/(n-1)
    starts=[]
    if existing: clip normalize
    # power inv sqrt with c
    for c in [0.25,1.0,4.0,16.0,64.0]: a=1/sqrt(j+c)
    # binomial recursion
    a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1; for i: a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i); maybe scale (large n no overflow? a decays ~1/√, starting 1; for i large product okay; no underflow until i? ~1/√i, fine)
    # powers alpha
    for alpha in [0.25,0.35,0.45,0.5,0.55,0.65]: a=(j+1)**(-alpha)
    # rational decreasing
    for lam in [0.5,1,2,3]: a=1/(1+lam*t)
    # exponentials
    for lam in [0.5,1,2,3,5]: a=np.exp(-lam*t)+1e-6
    # reversed equivalents automatically by reversing some start list
    # u-shape? a=1+cos(πt)? but symmetric not.
    # sine modulated invsqrt
    base=1/sqrt(j+0.5); a=base*(1+0.3 sin(2π k t + phase)) for k 2,5
    # uniform, ramp
    # random positive (exponential, dirichlet)
    # also use a constructed by optimizing low-dim param maybe not.

Let's systematically think continuous. We want h(t)=∫f(s)f(t-s). This is the distribution of sum of two iid random variables with density f. We want minimize the maximum point mass/pdf of the sum, given f nonnegative density on [0,1]. This is exactly: Among all distributions on [0,1], minimize the supremum of the convolution density of X+X. What is the minimal peak of the distribution of 2X? We can choose X not independent? Our sequence discrete approximates density, no atoms if values smooth; but could have spikes approximating atoms? A spike of small width high amplitude gives convolution spike at 0 and 2t, high. - Start μ uniform.
- Constraint violation for each j: v_j = A^T μ_j. Need v_j ≥1. If some v_j<1, increase μ on lags that contribute to j? This is like covering.
But we also want minimize M∑μ; for fixed M, optimal μ maybe small. This is a linear covering problem. We can solve approximately by **multiplicative weights**:
   μ ← μ * exp(η (1 - A^T μ)/M?) for low v.

Now, exact smooth objective and jac for L-BFGS:
```
def _lsmax_func(x, n, L, temp, factor=1.0):
    a = np.exp(x - x.max()); a /= a.sum()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    B = np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:2*n-1]
    bmax = B.max()
    if bmax <= 0: return 0.0, a, None, None
    z = (temp * (B - bmax)) / bmax? Wait βB = (temp/Bmax)*B. z=β(B-bmax)=temp*(B-bmax)/bmax. Range [-temp,0].
    expz = np.exp(z); S=expz.sum()
    F = 2*n*(bmax + (bmax/temp)*np.log(S))
    w = expz/S
    H = rfft(w,L)
    G = 2*irfft(conj(A)*H,L)[:n].real
    meanG = a @ G
    jac = 2*n*a*(G - meanG)
    return F, a, jac
```

`_weights_from_b(b, temp)`:

bmax=b.max()
if bmax<=0: return None
z = temp * (b - bmax) / bmax
# z in [-temp,0]
w = np.exp(z)  # for very negative underflows okay
wsum=w.sum()
if not finite or wsum<=0: w=np.full(len(b),1/len(b))
else w/=wsum
return w
```
This is softmax with βBmax=temp. For temp high, w top. For temp low, w nearly uniform (z near0) but as discussed, if temp=1, low lags with b=0 have e^{-1}=0.37, still significant; not uniform but okay.

Gradient:
```
def _grad_from_weights(a,w,A,L,n):
    H=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    G=2.0*np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*H,L)[:n].real
    return G
```
Check: `w` sum1; G scale ~2/n. Could also omit factor 2? Direction scale irrelevant due step; but gradient consistency maybe. Leave.

`_multiplicative_move(a,g,eta)`:
```
# g maybe centered
shift = np.dot(a,g)
d = -eta*(g - shift)
d -= d.max()
a_new = a*np.exp(d)
s=a_new.sum()
if finite and s>0: return a_new/s
return a
```
If eta huge, exp(d) might under/overflow; d range = eta*(g_range). We set eta=step_factor/g_range, so d range=step_factor, safe. If g_range=0, return a.

Line search in mirror:
```
def _try_mirror_step(a, B, w, G, bmax, eta_factor, L, n):
    # Use G centered
    meanG = np.dot(a,G)
    guse = G - meanG
    rng_g = guse.max()-guse.min()
    if rng_g < 1e-300: return None, bmax, eta_factor
    eta = eta_factor / max(rng_g, 1e-300)
    # current weighted objective
    Fcur = np.dot(w,B)
    for _ in range(6):
        a_try = _mult(a,guse,eta)
        A_try=np.fft.rfft(a_try,L)
        B_try=np.fft.irfft(A_try*A_try,L)[:2*n-1]
        b_try=B_try.max()
        Ftry=np.dot(w,B_try)
        if (Ftry < Fcur - 1e-12) or (b_try < bmax - 1e-12): # absolute
             # But if b_try improves but F not? accept
             return a_try, b_try, eta_factor
        # If b_try slightly worse but F much better? accept maybe
        if Ftry < Fcur - 1e-10 and b_try < bmax*(1+1e-6):
             return a_try,b_try,eta_factor
        eta *=0.5
    return None, bmax, eta_factor
```
Absolute tolerances: B scale ~1/n ~1e-4. Use relative: Fcur ~bmax. `tol = 1e-12*max(1, Fcur)`. If bmax ~1e-4, 1e-12. Accept if `< Fcur*(1-1e-10)` maybe. If bmax improves even tiny, accept. We want slow improvements; use `Ftry <= Fcur*(1-1e-10)` or `b_try < bmax*(1-1e-9)`. But line search may reject due to noise; okay.
Need update momentum before or after acceptance? We can compute Guse with momentum, line search. If accepted, update momentum with G (not Guse?) and increase factor. If rejected, reduce factor.

Momentum with centering:
```
m = beta*m + (1-beta)*G_center? Use G centered at current? G not centered maybe. For direction, use `gdir = G + beta*m`? Then center gdir.
```
Define:
- `G = _grad...`
- `Gc = G - dot(a,G)`
- update `m = beta*m + (1-beta)*Gc`
- `Guse = Gc + beta*m` (or `Gc + m`?) Then center again: `Guse -= dot(a,Guse)`.
Momentum scale: if beta=0.5, Guse includes past. Good. Reset on perturbation.

Perturb function:
```
def _perturb(a, rng, strength):
    n=len(a); j=np.arange(n)
    # random low-frequency smooth multiplier
    noise=np.zeros(n)
    ncomp=min(20, n//2)
    for k in range(1, ncomp+1):
        amp=strength* rng.normal(0,1.0)/np.sqrt(k)
        phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
        noise += amp * np.sin(2*np.pi*k*j/n + phase)
    # also exponential tilt
    if rng.random()<0.3: noise += strength*rng.uniform(-1,1)*(j/n-0.5)
    d=noise-noise.mean()
    a2=a*np.exp(d)
    return _normalize_positive(a2)
```
Strength maybe 0.1-0.5. When stuck, use 0.3.

def _polish_scipy(a, n, L, deadline, rng, label):
    x0 = np.log(a + 1e-300)
    x0 -= x0.max()
    best_x=x0.copy(); best_score=2*n*_b_max... normalized
    temps = [6,12,24,48,96]
    for temp in temps:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline-20: break

          if res.success or res.x finite:
             x0 = res.x - res.x.max()
             a_new = softmax(x0)

             update
       except: pass
    return best...

Alternative: implement custom **Adam** on logits using smooth objective exact gradient. This may be more stable and lighter than L-BFGS. For Adam/Adagrad:
- Maintain first/second moments of grad_theta.
- Update θ -= lr * m/(sqrt(v)+eps). lr maybe 0.01? Need scale.
Since gradient wrt θ scale ~a*G ~1/n^2=1e-8. To get O(1) log change, lr ~1000. We can normalize lr by gradient RMS: `lr = step / (rms+eps)`. This is similar to mirror but with second-order. Could be robust. Use exact smooth logsumexp objective (with temp annealing). Update θ (not a) and softmax. Line search? Adam may oscillate; we can monitor score. Implement `_optimize_smooth_adam`:
```
θ = log(a)
m=v=0
lr_factor=1.0
for it:
  a=softmax(θ); B; temp; z; S; w; G; meanG; gradθ=a*(G-meanG)
  # normalize grad by its max/range
  gscale = np.linalg.norm(gradθ)/sqrt(n) or max(abs)
  lr = lr_factor / (gscale+eps)
  m=β1*m + (1-β1)*gradθ
  v=β2*v +(1-β2)*gradθ^2
  θ -= lr * m_hat/(sqrt(v)+eps)
  # optional line search? Could just accept if score not worsen? We can track current score; if worsens, scale back.
```

Optimization function pseudo complete:
```
def _optimize_mirror(a0,n,L,deadline,rng,label=''):
    a=_normalize_positive(a0)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_b=_bmax(a,n,L)
    st_b=best_b
    m=np.zeros(n); beta=0.5
    temp=5.0; temp_end=80.0
    step=0.8
    stuck=0; total=0
    while time < deadline:
       total+=1
       # anneal temp based on total maybe use time
       temp = 5 + 75*min(1, total/3000)
       # occasional high temp?
       A=rfft(a,L); B=irfft(A*A,L)[:2n-1]; bmax=B.max()
       if bmax < best_b - 1e-15: best=...
       if best_b < st_b - 1e-12: st_b=best_b; stuck=0
       else: stuck += 1
       if stuck > 40:
           a=_perturb(best_a if rng.random()<0.5 else a, rng, 0.25)
           m*=0.1; st_b=best_b; stuck=0; step=max(0.6, step*0.8)
       w=_weights_from_b(B,temp)
       H=rfft(w,L); G=2*irfft(conj(A)*H,L)[:n].real
       Gc=G-dot(a,G)
       m=beta*m+(1-beta)*Gc
       Guse=Gc+beta*m
       Guse-=dot(a,Guse)
       g_range=Guse.max()-Guse.min()+1e-300
       accepted=False
       for trial in range(4):
          eta=step/g_range
          a_try=_mult(a,Guse,eta)
          A_try=rfft(a_try,L); B_try=irfft(A_try*A_try,L)[:2n-1]; b_try=B_try.max()
          Fcur=dot(w,B); Ftry=dot(w,B_try)
          # Determine accept
          if Ftry < Fcur*(1-1e-10) and b_try < bmax*(1+1e-7):
              a=a_try; accepted=True; break
          if b_try < bmax*(1-1e-9):
              a=a_try; accepted=True; break
          # If even small halving no, perhaps accept if Ftry reasonably close and btry not much worse to allow exploration?
          eta*=0.5
       if not accepted:
          # maybe try super-small step in same direction? If Ftry not decrease, direction bad due weights; maybe choose new weights? We recompute next.
          step *=0.5
          if step < 0.05:
             a=_perturb(a,rng,0.1)
             step=0.8
       else:
          if bmax < best_b: step=min(3, step*1.05)
          else: step=max(0.2, step*0.9)
    return best_a,best_b
```
But note `Fcur` computed inside trial loop; can compute before. We use B from top; B cosine.

Define `_lsmax_func(x, n, L, temp):` returns scalar. It should be robust.
```
def _lsmax_func(x, n, L, temp):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return 1e100
    x -= x.max()
    a = np.exp(x)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return 1e100
    a /= s
    F = np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    B = np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)[:2*n-1]
    B = np.maximum(B,0)
    bmax = float(B.max())
    if bmax <= 0: return 0.0
    z = temp * (B - bmax) / bmax
    ez = np.exp(z)
    S = ez.sum()
    if S<=0: return 2*n*bmax
    return float(2*n*(bmax + (bmax/temp)*np.log(S)))
```
`_lsmax_jac(x,n,L,temp)` similar compute jac. To avoid recomputing in func and jac, we can just let scipy call both; fine.
```
def _lsmax_jac(x,n,L,temp):
   ... same compute a, A, B, bmax, z, ez, S, w
   H=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
   G=2*np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*H,L)[:n].real
   meanG=np.dot(a,G)
   return 2*n*a*(G-meanG)
```
But objective includes the `+ (bmax/temp)logS` term. Derivative of bmax term not included! Wait smooth max logsumexp derivative is ∑w∂B only; the expression `Bmax + (Bmax/temp)logS` with Bmax = max(B) is not differentiable due to Bmax term. Oops! We used max(B) plus log of shifted exp; derivative of max(B) is not ∑w∂B. The smooth logsumexp objective is `(1/β) log ∑ e^{β B}`. If we subtract β bmax, we must use same bmax? `(1/β) log ∑ e^{β(B-bmax)} = (1/β) log ∑ e^{βB} - bmax`. This differs by -bmax (non-differentiable). So our F with +bmax is wrong derivative. Need use either full logsumexp including βbmax and bmax multiplicative? The exact smooth F = (1/β) log ∑ exp(βB). To compute stable: `logsumexp = β*bmax + np.log(S)`, then `F_raw = logsumexp/β = bmax + np.log(S)/β`. This is exactly what we wrote. Derivative of F = ∑w ∂B? Let's verify: F = (1/β) log(e^{β bmax}S) = bmax + (1/β)logS, but bmax is max(B), which is not differentiable. However in the expression, bmax appears inside log as β bmax; if we treat bmax as the actual max function, F is not differentiable. The standard stable logsumexp of vector βB is `M + log(∑ exp(βB-M))` with M = max(βB) = β bmax. This equals βbmax + logS. This is differentiable? The logsumexp of smooth functions is smooth, but using M=max for stability does not make it non-differentiable mathematically; the value β max(B)+log∑e^{β(B-Bmax)} is equal to log∑e^{βB}, which is smooth. The maximum term is just for computation; the sum's derivative accounts for the max term? Wait derivative of the **computed expression** with bmax treated as hard max is not the derivative of logsumexp because bmax is nondifferentiable, but the equality holds for all B; derivative of the smooth function log∑e^{βB} is ∑w∂B. At points where bmax ties, the representation has kinks? The logsumexp is smooth, but `max(B)+...` as a function of raw B maybe appears nonsmooth, yet the composition is smooth because the logS term cancels kinks? Let's test one-dimensional B=[x,y], β. F=(e^{βx}+e^{βy})/β? Actually (1/β)log(e^{βx}+e^{βy}). Stable: M=β max(x,y). If x>y: F=x+(1/β)log(1+e^{-β(x-y)}), derivative =1 - (e^{-βd}/(1+e^{-βd})) = 1/(1+e^{βd}) = w_x. Good; derivative of x term canceled. If we compute `bmax + logS/β` with S=1+e^{-βd}, derivative wrt x includes 1 + derivative logS =1 - β(1*S? Let's compute derivative logS/β: derivative S/S *(-1) -> - w_y, so total =1 - w_y = w_x. Good! So expression with max is differentiable and derivative includes derivative of max term plus logS; the formula derivative ∑w∂B holds. Our gradient using only G=∑w∂B is valid for logsumexp F_raw, not for a function that treats max as constant. Since mathematically F_raw=logsumexp/β = bmax + logS/β; its gradient is ∑w∂B. Good. So our objective value formula is correct and gradient formula correct. The apparent nondiff at ties is resolved. Good.

But the large constant logN/temp issue: logsumexp formula is correct; as β→0, (1/β)log∑e^{βB} diverges due logN/β. So smooth max with β small has large constant. Gradient correct.

For F raw scale: if temp=10, bmax/temp*logS maybe up to bmax/10*logN ~0.025*bmax; small. If temp=1, ~10*bmax. Fine.

Thus `_lsmax_func` OK. Gradient of F_score=2n*Fraw: `2n*a*(G - meanG)` where G=∑w∂B. Yes.

Need `_select_top_starts` with `given` include:
```
def _select_top_starts(starts, k):
    if not starts: return []
    s = sorted(starts, key=_start_sort_key)
    k=min(k,len(s))
    sel=s[:k]
    # ensure given/resampled
    for item in s:
        nm=item[0]
        if nm.startswith('given') or nm.startswith('resamp'):
            present=False
            for x in sel:
                if x is item:
                    present=True; break
            if not present:
                if len(sel)<k:
                    sel.append(item)
                else:
                    # replace last (worst among selected)
                    sel[-1]=item
            break
    return sel
```
Since `item` lists; identity okay.

`_optimize_mirror` details:
```
def _optimize_mirror(...):
    a=_normalize_positive(a0)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_b=_b_max_fft(a,n,L)
    ref_b=best_b
    m=np.zeros(n); beta=0.5
    step=step_init
    stuck=0
    it=0
    # if deadline not > now return
    while True:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       it +=1
       temp = temp_start + (temp_end-temp_start)*min(1.0, it/5000.0)
       # occasional random temp
       if rng.random() < 0.02:
           temp = rng.uniform(max(temp,10), temp_end)
       A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
       B=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:2*n-1]
       B=np.maximum(B,0.0)
       bmax=float(B.max())
       if bmax < best_b - 1e-15:
           best_b=bmax; best_a=a.copy()
       if best_b < ref_b - 1e-12:
           ref_b=best_b; stuck=0
       else:
           stuck +=1
       if stuck > max_stuck:
           if use_perturb:
              if rng.random()<0.5:
                  a=_smooth_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.25)
              else:
                  a=_smooth_perturb(a, rng, 0.35)
              m*=0.1; step=max(0.5, step*0.8); ref_b=best_b; stuck=0
           else:
              step = max(0.05, step*0.7)
              # maybe reset temp
              if rng.random()<0.5: temp=temp_end
       w=_weights_from_b(B,temp)
       if w is None: break
       G=_grad_from_weights(a,w,A,L,n)
       Gc=G-float(np.dot(a,G))
       m=beta*m+(1-beta)*Gc
       Guse=Gc+beta*m
       Guse=Guse-float(np.dot(a,Guse))
       g_range=float(Guse.max()-Guse.min())
       if g_range < 1e-300:
           step*=0.5
           continue
       Fcur=float(np.dot(w,B))
       accepted=False
       eta=step/g_range
       for _ in range(5):
           a_try=_mult_move(a,Guse,eta)
           A_try=np.fft.rfft(a_try,L)
           B_try=np.fft.irfft(A_try*A_try,L)[:2*n-1]
           B_try=np.maximum(B_try,0.0)
           b_try=float(B_try.max())
           Ftry=float(np.dot(w,B_try))
           # accept if weighted sum decreased or peak decreased
           if Ftry <= Fcur*(1.0-1e-9) and b_try <= bmax*(1.0+1e-7):
               a=a_try; accepted=True; break
           if b_try < bmax*(1.0-1e-10):
               a=a_try; accepted=True; break
           # If Ftry decreased slightly but btry worsened slightly, maybe accept? We already first condition allows btry +1e-7.
           eta *= 0.5
       if not accepted:
           step *= 0.5
           if step < 0.03:
              step = 0.5
              if use_perturb:
                 a=_smooth_perturb(a,rng,0.1)
                 m*=0.2
              else:
                 # recovery: restore best
                 a=best_a.copy()
          if bmax < best_b: step = min(3.0, step*1.03)
          else: step = max(0.2, step*0.97)
    # final score
    final_b=_b_max_fft(a,n,L)
    if final_b < best_b: best_a=a; best_b=final_b
    return best_a, best_b
```
Note in else after not accepted, indentation; then if accepted? Need code.

`_mult_move`: If g centered, zero dot. If step factor/range, eta maybe large but okay.

`_smooth_perturb`: preserve sum; use floor.
```
def _smooth_perturb(a, rng, strength):
    n=len(a); if n<2: return a.copy()
    j=np.arange(n)
    noise=np.zeros(n)
    ncomp=min(25, n//2)
    for k in range(1,ncomp+1):
       amp=strength* rng.normal(0.0,1.0)/np.sqrt(float(k))
       phase=rng.uniform(0.0,2*np.pi)
       noise += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*k*j/float(n) + phase)
    # optional tilt
    if rng.random()<0.3:
       noise += strength* rng.uniform(-1,1)*(j/float(n)-0.5)
    noise -= noise.mean()
    a2=a*np.exp(noise)
    return _normalize_positive(a2)
```

`_polish_scipy`:
```
def _polish_scipy(a0,n,L,deadline,rng):
   if _scipy_minimize is None: return a0, _score_fast...
   a=_normalize_positive(a0)
   best_a=a.copy(); best_b=_b_max_fft(a,n,L)
   rng? not used
   temps=[8,18,40,80]
   for temp in temps:
      if time.monotonic() >= deadline-10: break
      x = np.log(a + 1e-300); x -= x.max()
      # maybe try two runs with small random noise
      for attempt in range(2):
          if time.monotonic() >= deadline-10: break
          x0 = x.copy()
          if attempt==1: x0 += rng.normal(0,0.05,n)
          try:
             res = _scipy_minimize(_lsmax_func, x0, args=(n,L,temp), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=_lsmax_jac, options={'maxiter':40, 'maxfun':200, 'ftol':1e-13, 'gtol':1e-9})
          except Exception:
             res=None
          if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x'):
             x1=res.x
             x1=np.asarray(x1,float); x1-=x1.max(); a_new=np.exp(x1); s=a_new.sum(); a_new/=s
             b_new=_b_max_fft(a_new,n,L)
             if b_new < best_b - 1e-15:
                best_b=b_new; best_a=a_new.copy(); a=a_new.copy()
             else:
                # if no improvement, keep x0? maybe a unchanged
                pass
          x = np.log(_normalize_positive(best_a)+1e-300); x-=x.max()
   return best_a,best_b
```

def _search_one_length(n, existing, deadline, rng):
   if n <=0: return None, inf
   L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
   starts=_make_starts(n,rng,existing)
   if not starts: return np.ones(n)/n, _score_fast(...)
   sel=_select_top_starts(starts, 6)
   local_best_seq=None; local_best_sc=inf
   # initial update from selected?
   for item in sel: if item[2]<local: ...
   # time allocation
   base = max(20, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.75 / max(1,len(sel)))
   for idx,item in enumerate(sel):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline-60: break
       alloc = base * (1.0 - 0.08*idx)
       d = time.monotonic() + alloc
       if d > deadline-60: d = deadline-60
       if d - time.monotonic() < 10: continue
       a,b = _optimize_mirror(item[1], n, L, d, rng, label=item[0])
       sc=2*n*b
       if sc < local_best_sc:
           local_best_sc=sc; local_best_seq=a.copy()
   # polish
   if local_best_seq is None: local_best_seq=sel[0][1]
   if time.monotonic() < deadline-80:
       polish_d = time.monotonic() + min(90, (deadline - time.monotonic())*0.35)
       a,b = _optimize_mirror(local_best_seq,n,L,polish_d,rng,label='polish',temp_start=25,temp_end=100,step_init=0.3,use_perturb=False,max_stuck=80)
       sc=... update
   # scipy
   if _scipy_minimize is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline-50:
       spd = time.monotonic() + min(150, (deadline - time.monotonic())*0.5)
       a,b = _polish_scipy(local_best_seq,n,L,spd,rng)
       update
       # after scipy, mirror polish
       if time.monotonic() < deadline-20:
          md = deadline-20
          a,b=_optimize_mirror(local_best_seq,n,L,md,rng,label='post',temp_start=30,temp_end=120,step_init=0.2,use_perturb=False,max_stuck=100)
          update
   return local_best_seq, local_best_sc
```

Compute `_weights_from_b` can also return `S`? It computes exp(z). We can combine:
```
def _soft_weights_value(b,temp):
   bmax=b.max(); if bmax<=0: return uniform, 0.0
   z=temp*(b-bmax)/bmax
   ez=np.exp(z)
   S=ez.sum()
   w=ez/S
   F=bmax + bmax/temp*np.log(S)
   return w,F
```
Use in optimizer and maybe not in scipy (scipy has own). This avoids recompute exp twice.

```python
"""
Step-function optimizer for
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a))^2

with a non-negative sequence.

Strategy
--------
The main search uses a smooth max-autocorrelation objective
    (1/beta) log(sum exp(beta * conv(a,a)_k))
optimized by multiplicative/mirror steps in the simplex.
The gradient is computed by FFT correlation.  The method adapts the
temperature beta, uses momentum, line-searching, and smooth low-frequency
perturbations to escape local minima.  Additional polishing is done with
a short scipy L-BFGS-B stage on the same smooth objective.

The code tries several analytical/parametric starts, including reciprocal
square-root shapes, binomial-square-root shapes, exponentials, and the
provided global sequence when present.  It may also explore a second
sequence length if enough time remains.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = len(a)
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    a /= s
    # Tiny floor so multiplicative updates can revive near-zero positions.
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-18)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    return a / s


def _b_max_fft(a, n, L):
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    B = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
    return float(B.max())


def _score_fast(a, n, L):
    return float(2.0 * n * _b_max_fft(a, n, L))


def _soft_weights_value(b, temp):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    bmax = float(b.max())
    if bmax <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(bmax):
        nll = max(1, len(b))
        return np.full(nll, 1.0 / nll, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    z = temp * (b - bmax) / bmax
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    S = float(ez.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
        return np.full(len(b), 1.0 / len(b), dtype=np.float64), bmax
    w = ez / S
    # Smooth max value: (1/beta) log sum exp(beta b), stable form.
    F = bmax + (bmax / temp) * float(np.log(S))
    return w, F


def _grad_from_weights(a, w, A, L, n):
    H = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    G = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A) * H, L)[:n].real
    return G


def _mult_move(a, g, eta):
    g = g - float(np.dot(a, g))
    d = -eta * g
    d -= d.max()
    a2 = a * np.exp(d)
    s = float(a2.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return a
    return a2 / s


def _smooth_perturb(a, rng, strength):
    n = len(a)
    if n < 2:
        return a.copy()
    j = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    ncomp = min(25, n // 2)
    for k in range(1, ncomp + 1):
        amp = strength * float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)) / np.sqrt(float(k))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        noise += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * j / float(n) + phase)
    if rng.random() < 0.30:
        noise += strength * float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)) * (j / float(n) - 0.5)
    noise -= float(noise.mean())
    return _normalize_positive(a * np.exp(noise))


def _resample_sequence(seq, n):
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    if float(arr.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m = arr.size
    if m == n:
        return arr
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(arr[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, float(m - 1), n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, arr)
    return _normalize_positive(out)


def _add_start(starts, a, name):
    a = _normalize_positive(a)
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 0:
        return
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    sc = _score_fast(a, n, L)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < 50.0:
        starts.append([name, a, float(sc)])


def _make_starts(n, rng, existing=None):
    starts = []
    if n <= 0:
        return starts

    if existing is not None:
        ex = np.asarray(existing, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if ex.size > 0 and np.any(np.isfinite(ex)) and float(ex.sum()) > 0:
            if ex.size == n:
                _add_start(starts, ex, "given")
            else:
                _add_start(starts, _resample_sequence(ex, n), "resampled")

    j = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = j / float(max(1, n - 1))
    jm = j + 0.5

    # Reciprocal square-root family. Important at the optimum scale.
    for c in (0.0, 0.25, 1.0, 4.0, 16.0, 64.0, 256.0):
        _add_start(starts, 1.0 / np.sqrt(jm + c), "isqrt_" + str(c))

    # Coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), i.e. the formal square root of a flat
    # convolution profile.
    ab = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    ab[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        ab[i] = ab[i - 1] * float(2 * i - 1) / float(2 * i)
    _add_start(starts, ab, "binom_sqrt")

    # Power laws.
    for alpha in (0.25, 0.35, 0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.65, 0.80):
        _add_start(starts, (j + 1.0) ** (-alpha), "pow_" + str(alpha))

    # Rational decreasing/increasing shapes.
    for c in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0, 8.0):
        _add_start(starts, 1.0 / (1.0 + c * t), "ratdec_" + str(c))
        _add_start(starts, 1.0 / (1.0 + c * (1.0 - t)), "ratinc_" + str(c))

    # Exponentials.
    for lam in (0.4, 0.8, 1.5, 2.5, 4.0, 7.0, 12.0):
        _add_start(starts, np.exp(-lam * t) + 1e-6, "expdec_" + str(lam))
        _add_start(starts, np.exp(-lam * (1.0 - t)) + 1e-6, "expinc_" + str(lam))

    # Smooth parametric multiplicative modulations of the square-root shape.
    base = 1.0 / np.sqrt(jm)
    for beta in np.linspace(-0.9, 0.9, 13):
        _add_start(starts, base * np.exp(beta * t), "isqrt_expbeta_" + str(int(beta * 100)))
        _add_start(starts, np.maximum(base * (1.0 + beta * t), 1e-9), "isqrt_linbeta_" + str(int(beta * 100)))

    # Low-frequency sinusoidal modulations.
    for k in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, 21):
        for amp in (0.08, 0.18, 0.35):
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            _add_start(starts, base * (1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + phase)),
                       "isqrt_sin_k%d_a%d" % (k, int(amp * 100)))

    # U-shaped and mid-hump windows.
    for c in (1.0, 4.0, 10.0, 25.0):
        _add_start(starts, 1.0 + c * (t - 0.5) ** 2, "ushape_" + str(c))
        _add_start(starts, np.maximum(1.0 - c * (t - 0.5) ** 2, 1e-3) + 1e-4, "hump_" + str(c))

    # Simple random starts.
    for r in range(4):
        _add_start(starts, rng.exponential(1.0, size=n), "rand_exp_" + str(r))
    if n < 20000:
        for r in range(2):
            _add_start(starts, rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n) * 0.05), "rand_dirichlet_" + str(r))

    if not starts:
        starts.append(["uniform", np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n), 2.0])
    return starts


def _start_sort_key(item):
    return item[2] if np.isfinite(item[2]) else 1e100


def _select_top_starts(starts, k):
    if not starts:
        return []
    s = sorted(starts, key=_start_sort_key)
    k = max(1, min(k, len(s)))
    sel = s[:k]
    for item in s:
        name = item[0]
        if name.startswith("given") or name.startswith("resampled"):
            present = False
            for x in sel:
                if x is item:
                    present = True
                    break
            if not present:
                if len(sel) < k:
                    sel.append(item)
                else:
                    sel[-1] = item
            break
    return sel


def _optimize_mirror(a0, n, L, deadline, rng, label="",
                     temp_start=5.0, temp_end=80.0,
                     step_init=0.8, use_perturb=True, max_stuck=40):
    a = _normalize_positive(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_b = _b_max_fft(a, n, L)
    ref_b = best_b

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    beta = 0.5
    step = float(step_init)
    stuck = 0
    it = 0

    while True:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        it += 1

        progress = min(1.0, it / 5000.0)
        temp = temp_start + (temp_end - temp_start) * progress
        if rng.random() < 0.03:
            temp = float(rng.uniform(max(10.0, temp), max(temp + 5.0, temp_end)))

        A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        B = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:2 * n - 1]
        B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
        bmax = float(B.max())

        if bmax < best_b - 1e-16:
            best_b = bmax
            best_a = a.copy()

        if best_b < ref_b - 1e-12:
            ref_b = best_b
            stuck = 0
        else:
            stuck += 1

        if stuck > max_stuck:
            if use_perturb:
                if rng.random() < 0.55:
                    a = _smooth_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.25)
                else:
                    a = _smooth_perturb(a, rng, 0.40)
                m *= 0.1
                step = max(0.5, step * 0.75)
                ref_b = best_b
                stuck = 0
            else:
                step = max(0.05, step * 0.7)
                temp = max(temp, temp_end)

        w, Fcur = _soft_weights_value(B, temp)
        if Fcur <= 1e-300:
            break

        G = _grad_from_weights(a, w, A, L, n)
        Gc = G - float(np.dot(a, G))
        m = beta * m + (1.0 - beta) * Gc
        Guse = m - float(np.dot(m, a))

        g_range = float(Guse.max() - Guse.min())
        if g_range < 1e-300:
            step *= 0.5
            continue

        eta = float(step) / g_range
        accepted = False
        ftol = max(1e-18, 1e-11 * abs(Fcur))

        for _ in range(5):
            a_try = _mult_move(a, Guse, eta)
            A_try = np.fft.rfft(a_try, L)
            B_try = np.fft.irfft(A_try * A_try, L)[:2 * n - 1]
            B_try = np.maximum(B_try, 0.0)
            b_try = float(B_try.max())
            _, Ftry = _soft_weights_value(B_try, temp)

            if Ftry <= Fcur - ftol and b_try <= bmax * (1.0 + 1e-8):
                a = a_try
                accepted = True
                break
            if b_try < bmax - max(1e-18, 1e-11 * bmax):
                a = a_try
                accepted = True
                break

            eta *= 0.5

        if accepted:
            step = min(3.0, step * 1.03)
        else:
            step *= 0.5
            if step < 0.03:
                step = 0.5
                if use_perturb:
                    a = _smooth_perturb(a, rng, 0.12)
                    m *= 0.2
                else:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

    final_b = _b_max_fft(a, n, L)
    if final_b < best_b:
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_b = final_b
    return best_a, best_b


def _lsmax_func(x, n, L, temp):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100
    x = x - x.max()
    a = np.exp(x)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return 1e100
    a /= s
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    B = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
    bmax = float(B.max())
    if bmax <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(bmax):
        return 0.0
    z = temp * (B - bmax) / bmax
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    S = float(np.exp(z).sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
        return float(2.0 * n * bmax)
    val = bmax + (bmax / temp) * float(np.log(S))
    return float(2.0 * n * val)


def _lsmax_jac(x, n, L, temp):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = x - x.max()
    a = np.exp(x)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a /= s
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    B = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
    bmax = float(B.max())
    if bmax <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(bmax):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = temp * (B - bmax) / bmax
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    S = float(ez.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = ez / S
    H = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    G = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A) * H, L)[:n].real
    meanG = float(np.dot(a, G))
    return 2.0 * float(n) * a * (G - meanG)


def _polish_scipy(a0, n, L, deadline, rng):
    if _scipy_minimize is None:
        return _normalize_positive(a0), _b_max_fft(a0, n, L)

    a = _normalize_positive(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_b = _b_max_fft(a, n, L)

    temps = (8.0, 18.0, 40.0, 80.0)
    for temp in temps:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 12:
            break
        for attempt in range(2):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 12:
                break
            x0 = np.log(a + 1e-300)
            x0 -= x0.max()
            if attempt == 1:
                x0 = x0 + rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, size=n)
            try:
                res = _scipy_minimize(
                    _lsmax_func,
                    x0,
                    args=(n, L, temp),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    jac=_lsmax_jac,
                    options={
                        "maxiter": 35,
                        "maxfun": 180,
                        "ftol": 1e-13,
                        "gtol": 1e-9,
                        "maxls": 20,
                    },
                )
            except Exception:
                res = None
            if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                x1 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                if np.all(np.isfinite(x1)):
                    x1 = x1 - x1.max()
                    a_new = np.exp(x1)
                    a_new = _normalize_positive(a_new)
                    b_new = _b_max_fft(a_new, n, L)
                    if b_new < best_b - 1e-16:
                        best_b = b_new
                        best_a = a_new.copy()
                        a = a_new.copy()
        a = best_a

    return best_a, best_b


def _search_one_length(n, existing, deadline, rng):
    if n <= 0:
        return None, float("inf")
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    starts = _make_starts(n, rng, existing)
    if not starts:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return a, _score_fast(a, n, L)

    selected = _select_top_starts(starts, 6)

    local_best_seq = None
    local_best_sc = float("inf")
    for item in selected:
        if item[2] < local_best_sc:
            local_best_sc = item[2]
            local_best_seq = item[1].copy()

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now < deadline - 30:
        base_alloc = max(10.0, (deadline - now) * 0.72 / max(1, len(selected)))
        for idx, item in enumerate(selected):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= deadline - 45:
                break
            alloc = base_alloc * (1.0 - 0.08 * idx)
            d = now + alloc
            if d > deadline - 45:
                d = deadline - 45
            if d - now < 8.0:
                continue
            print("  optimizing start %s initial_score=%.7f budget=%.0fs" %
                  (item[0], item[2], alloc), flush=True)
            a, b = _optimize_mirror(item[1], n, L, d, rng, label=item[0])
            sc = 2.0 * float(n) * float(b)
            if sc < local_best_sc:
                local_best_sc = sc
                local_best_seq = _normalize_positive(a)
                print("    new local best %.7f" % local_best_sc, flush=True)

    if local_best_seq is None:
        local_best_seq = selected[0][1].copy()
        local_best_sc = selected[0][2]

    # Gentle polish with small perturbations and high temperature.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if now < deadline - 70:
        rem = deadline - now
        polish_d = now + min(120.0, rem * 0.30)
        if polish_d > now + 20:
            a, b = _optimize_mirror(
                local_best_seq, n, L, polish_d, rng, label="polish",
                temp_start=25.0, temp_end=110.0,
                step_init=0.3, use_perturb=False, max_stuck=100
            )
            sc = 2.0 * float(n) * float(b)
            if sc < local_best_sc:
                local_best_sc = sc
                local_best_seq = _normalize_positive(a)
                print("    polish local best %.7f" % local_best_sc, flush=True)

    # Optional scipy L-BFGS-B polish.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if _scipy_minimize is not None and now < deadline - 60:
        rem = deadline - now
        spd = now + min(220.0, rem * 0.45)
        if spd > now + 25:
            a, b = _polish_scipy(local_best_seq, n, L, spd, rng)
            sc = 2.0 * float(n) * float(b)
            if sc < local_best_sc:
                local_best_sc = sc
                local_best_seq = _normalize_positive(a)
                print("    scipy local best %.7f" % local_best_sc, flush=True)
            # Re-mirror after scipy to convert smooth improvements into hard max improvements.
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now < deadline - 25:
                rem = deadline - now
                md = now + min(70.0, rem * 0.5)
                if md > now + 8:
                    a, b = _optimize_mirror(
                        local_best_seq, n, L, md, rng, label="post_scipy",
                        temp_start=35.0, temp_end=140.0,
                        step_init=0.2, use_perturb=False, max_stuck=120
                    )
                    sc = 2.0 * float(n) * float(b)
                    if sc < local_best_sc:
                        local_best_sc = sc
                        local_best_seq = _normalize_positive(a)
                        print("    post-scipy local best %.7f" % local_best_sc, flush=True)

    return local_best_seq, local_best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    global_deadline = t0 + budget_s - 30.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 90210)

    try:
        existing = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        existing = None

    ex_arr = None
    n_main = None
    if existing is not None:
        ex_arr = np.asarray(existing, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if ex_arr.size > 0 and np.any(np.isfinite(ex_arr)) and float(ex_arr.sum()) > 0:
            n0 = len(ex_arr)
            if n0 > 12000:
                n_main = 8192
                ex_arr = _resample_sequence(ex_arr, n_main)
            else:
                n_main = n0
                if n_main < 1024:
                    n_main = 4096
                    ex_arr = _resample_sequence(ex_arr, n_main)
        elif ex_arr.size > 0:
            # Existing may be invalid; try again from scratch.
            ex_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(ex_arr), ex_arr, 0.0)
            ex_arr = np.maximum(ex_arr, 0.0)
            if float(ex_arr.sum()) > 0:
                n_main = len(ex_arr) if len(ex_arr) >= 512 else 4096
                ex_arr = _resample_sequence(ex_arr, n_main)
            else:
                ex_arr = None

    if n_main is None:
        n_main = 4096
        ex_arr = None

    print("main length n=%d" % n_main, flush=True)

    # Reserve time for a possible secondary length.
    if budget_s > 1500:
        reserve_secondary = 300.0
    elif budget_s > 800:
        reserve_secondary = 150.0
    else:
        reserve_secondary = 70.0
    main_deadline = min(global_deadline - reserve_secondary, global_deadline)

    best_seq, best_score = _search_one_length(n_main, ex_arr, main_deadline, rng)
    if best_seq is not None:
        print("main length best score=%.7f" % best_score, flush=True)

    # Optional second length.  Larger length often lowers the asymptotic score,
    # but is more expensive.
    remaining = global_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 260.0 and best_seq is not None:
        if n_main < 8192:
            n_secondary = 8192
        else:
            n_secondary = 4096
        if n_secondary != n_main:
            ex_secondary = _resample_sequence(ex_arr, n_secondary) if ex_arr is not None else None
            print("secondary length n=%d" % n_secondary, flush=True)
            seq2, sc2 = _search_one_length(n_secondary, ex_secondary, global_deadline - 20.0, rng)
            if seq2 is not None and sc2 < best_score:
                best_seq = seq2
                best_score = sc2
                print("secondary length best score=%.7f" % best_score, flush=True)

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = np.ones(n_main, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_main)
        best_score = 2.0

    best_seq = _normalize_positive(best_seq)

    # Final validation with the official evaluator on a few candidates.
    candidates = []
    if best_seq is not None:
        candidates.append(("final_best", best_seq))
    if ex_arr is not None:
        candidates.append(("given_long", _normalize_positive(ex_arr)))
    candidates.append(("uniform_best_len", np.ones(len(best_seq), dtype=np.float64) / float(len(best_seq))))

    official_best_seq = best_seq
    official_best_score = best_score

    for name, cand in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() >= global_deadline - 5.0:
            break
        try:
            cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            if cand.size == 0:
                continue
            cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(cand.sum())
            if s < 0.01:
                cand = np.ones(cand.size, dtype=np.float64)
                cand /= cand.size
            payload = [float(x) for x in cand]
            sc = evaluate_sequence(payload)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < official_best_score:
                official_best_score = float(sc)
                official_best_seq = cand
                print("official candidate %s score=%.7f" % (name, official_best_score), flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    official_best_seq = np.clip(np.asarray(official_best_seq, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)
    if official_best_seq.size == 0 or float(official_best_seq.sum()) < 0.01:
        official_best_seq = np.ones(n_main, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_main)

    print("FINAL estimated score=%.7f n=%d time=%.1fs" %
          (official_best_score, official_best_seq.size, time.monotonic() - t0), flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in official_best_seq]
```
